NON-IFRS FINANCIAL MEASURES                                              非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標
In evaluating the business, MINISO considers and uses adjusted           於評估業務時，名創優品考慮並使用經調整經營利
operating profit, adjusted net profit, adjusted EBITDA and adjusted      潤、經調整淨利潤、經調整EBITDA和經調整每股基
basic and diluted net earnings per Share as supplemental measures        本及攤薄淨盈利作為審查及評估其經營業績的補充
to review and assess its operating performance. The presentation of      指標。呈列此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標並
these non-IFRS financial measures is not intended to be considered       非旨在視為獨立於或替代根據《國際財務報告準則》
in isolation or as a substitute for the financial information prepared   編製及呈列的財務資料。名創優品將經調整經營利
and presented in accordance with IFRS. MINISO defines adjusted           潤定義為不包括以權益結算的股份支付開支的期內
operating profit as operating profit for the period excluding equity-    經營利潤。名創優品將經調整淨利潤定義為不包括
settled share-based payment expenses. MINISO defines adjusted            以權益結算的股份支付開支、衍生工具公允價值變
net profit as profit for the period excluding equity-settled share-      動收益或損失、衍生工具的發行成本、與股票掛鈎
based payment expenses, gain or loss from fair value change of           證券（定義見下文）及與用於收購永輝超市股份有限
derivatives, issuance cost of derivatives and interest expenses          公司（「永輝」）股權的銀行貸款有關的利息開支、贖
related to the Equity Linked Securities (as defined below) and           回優先股的負債公允價值變動以及分佔永輝虧損，
interest expenses related to the bank loans used for acquisition of      稅後淨額的期內利潤。名創優品將經調整EBITDA界
the equity interest in Yonghui Superstores Co., Ltd (永輝超市股份有             定為經調整淨利潤加折舊及攤銷、財務成本（不包括
限公司) (“Yonghui”), changes in fair value of redemption liabilities        與股票掛鈎證券及與用於收購永輝股權的銀行貸款
arising from preferred shares and share of loss of Yonghui, net of       有關的利息開支）及所得稅開支。名創優品以本公司
tax. MINISO defines adjusted EBITDA as adjusted net profit plus          權益股東應佔經調整淨利潤除以按《國際財務報告準
depreciation and amortization, finance costs excluding interest          則》基準計算每股基本及攤薄盈利所用的股份數量
expenses related to the Equity Linked Securities and interest            計算經調整每股基本及攤薄淨盈利。名創優品以與
expenses related to the bank loans used for acquisition of the           計算經調整每股美國存託股（定義見下文）基本及攤
equity interest in Yonghui and income tax expense. MINISO                薄淨盈利同樣的方式計算經調整每股基本及攤薄淨
computes adjusted basic and diluted net earnings per Share by            盈利，惟其使用按《國際財務報告準則》基準計算每
dividing adjusted net profit attributable to the equity shareholders     股基本及攤薄盈利所用的普通股數量作為分母，而
of the Company by the number of Shares used in the basic and             非此等普通股所代表的美國存託股數量。
diluted earnings per Share calculation on an IFRS basis. MINISO
computes adjusted basic and diluted net earnings per Share in the
same way as it calculates adjusted basic and diluted net earnings
per ADS (as defined below), except that it uses the number of
Shares used in the basic and diluted earnings per Share calculation
on an IFRS basis as the denominator instead of the number of
ADSs represented by these Shares.

MINISO presents these non-IFRS financial measures because they           名創優品呈報此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指
are used by the management to evaluate its operating performance         標，因管理層以此等指標評估其經營業績及制定業
and formulate business plans. These non-IFRS financial measures          務計劃。此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標使管
enable the management to assess its operating results without            理層可在不考慮名創優品認為並非未來營運表現指
considering the impacts of the aforementioned non-cash and other         標的上述非現金及其他調整項目影響的情況下評估
adjustment items that MINISO does not consider to be indicative          其經營業績。因此，名創優品認為使用此等非《國際
of its operating performance in the future. Accordingly, MINISO          財務報告準則》財務指標為投資者及其他人士以與
believes that the use of these non-IFRS financial measures provides      管理層及董事會相同的方式理解及評估其經營業績
useful information to investors and others in understanding              提供了有用的資料。
and evaluating its operating results in the same manner as the
management and the Board.

MINISO’s operations. Further, these non-IFRS financial measures            響名創優品營運的所有收入及開支項目。此外，此

including peer companies, and therefore their comparability may be         公司（包括同行公司）使用的非《國際財務報告準則》

 BUSINESS REVIEW FOR THE REPORTING                                        報告期業務回顧
 PERIOD
We are a global high-growth value retailer offering a variety of          我們是一家提供以獨特IP設計為特色的豐富多樣的
trendy lifestyle products featuring distinctive IP designs. Since         潮流生活產品的全球高增長價值零售商。自2013年
opening our first store in Chinese mainland in 2013, we have              在中國內地開設第一家門店以來，我們已成功打造
successfully built two brands – “MINISO” and “TOP TOY”. Our               了兩個品牌，分別是「名創優品」和「TOP TOY」。我
flagship brand “MINISO” has grown into a globally recognized              們的旗艦品牌「名創優品」已發展成為全球認可的零
retail brand that offers a frequently-refreshed assortment of lifestyle   售品牌，通過全球廣泛的門店網絡提供種類繁多及
products through an extensive store network worldwide. Our                高頻上新的生活產品。我們的產品可滿足消費者多
products cover diverse consumer needs and consumers are drawn             樣化的需求，名創優品以時尚、創意、高品質及高
to MINISO for our products’ trendiness, creativeness, high quality        性價比的產品吸引消費者。
and affordability.

Store operations in overseas markets                                 在海外市場的門店運營
We have adopted flexible store operation models, including direct    我們在拓展全球門店網絡的同時，根據市場的增長
operation, Retail Partner model and distributor model as we expand   潛力、當地法規和其他因素，採用了一系列靈活的
our global networks, depending on the growth potential, local        門店運營模式，包括直營、合夥人模式和代理模
regulation and other factors in the markets. In consideration of     式。考慮到當地不斷變化的監管要求、市場條件及
the evolving local regulatory requirements, market conditions and    營運需求，我們的海外加盟商有時會從合夥人轉變
their operational needs, our overseas franchisees may sometimes      為代理商，反之亦然。
convert from a Retail Partner to a distributor, or vice versa.

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                            主席兼首席執行官
Code provision C.2.1 of the Corporate Governance Code                           《企業管治守則》的守則條文第C.2.1條建議（但並無
recommends, but does not require, that the roles of chairman of the             規定）董事會主席與行政總裁的角色應有區分且不應
Board and chief executive officer should be separate and should                 由同一人兼任。
not be performed by the same individual.

The Company deviates from this code provision as we do not                      本公司偏離該守則條文，因為我們並無單獨的主席
have a separate chairman and chief executive officer and Mr. Ye                 與首席執行官，葉先生現兼任本公司這兩個職務。
currently performs these two roles of the Company. Mr. Ye is our                葉先生為我們的創始人，深諳我們的業務營運及管
founder and has extensive experience in our business operations                 理。董事會認為，主席與首席執行官由同一人士兼
and management. The Board believes that vesting the roles of                    任可確保本集團內部的一致領導，並使本集團的整
both chairperson and chief executive officer in the same person                 體戰略規劃更為有效及高效。董事會認為現時的安
has the benefit of ensuring consistent leadership within our Group              排並不會損害權力及權責的平衡，而此結構將確保
and enables more effective and efficient overall strategic planning             本公司迅速及有效地作出及執行決定。董事會將在
for our Group. The Board considers that the balance of power and                考慮本集團的整體情況後，適時繼續審議及考慮區
authority for the present arrangement will not be impaired and                  分董事會主席與本公司首席執行官的職責。
this structure will enable our Company to make and implement
decisions promptly and effectively. The Board will continue to
review and consider splitting the roles of chairman of the Board
and the chief executive officer of our Company if and when it is
appropriate taking into account the circumstances of the Group as
a whole.

BOARD MEETINGS                                                          董事會會議
The Company adopts the practice of holding Board meetings               本公司採納定期舉行董事會會議的慣例，每年至少
regularly, at least four times a year, and at approximately quarterly   舉行四次會議，大約每季舉行一次。本公司就所有
intervals. Notices of not less than 14 days are given for all regular   定期董事會會議發出不少於14日的通知，以向全體
Board meetings to provide all Directors with an opportunity to          董事提供機會出席定期會議並將有關事項納入議程。
attend and include matters in the agenda for a regular meeting.

For other Board meetings and Board Committee meetings,                  就其他董事會會議及董事委員會會議而言，本公司
reasonable notice is generally given by the Company. The agenda         一般會發出合理通知。議程及隨附會議文件於董事
and accompanying meeting papers are provided to the Directors           會會議或董事委員會會議舉行前至少三天提供予董
or Board Committee members at least three days before the Board         事或董事委員會成員，以確保董事有充足時間審閱
meetings or Board Committee meetings to ensure that the Directors       有關文件及充分準備出席董事會會議或董事委員會
have sufficient time to review the papers and be adequately             會議。倘董事或董事委員會成員未能出席會議，則
prepared for the Board meetings or Board Committee meetings.            彼等會獲悉將予討論的事宜，並有機會於會議召開
When Directors or Board Committee members are unable to attend          前知會主席有關彼等的意見。會議記錄由聯席公司
a meeting, they will be advised of the matters to be discussed and      秘書保存，副本將於全體董事間傳閱，以供參考及
given an opportunity to make their views known to the chairman          記錄。
prior to the meeting. Minutes of meetings are kept by the joint

Minutes of the Board meetings and Board Committee meetings are          董事會會議及董事委員會會議的會議記錄會充足詳
recorded in sufficient detail on the matters considered by the Board    盡記錄董事會及董事委員會所考慮的事項及所達致
and the Board Committees and the decisions reached, including           的決定，包括董事提出的任何關注。各董事會會議
any concerns raised by the Directors. Draft minutes of each Board       及董事委員會會議的會議記錄草擬本將於會議舉行

for comments within a reasonable time after the date on which the       意見。董事會會議記錄可供全體董事查閱。
meeting is held. The minutes of the Board meetings are open for

THE BOARD
The Board is the primary decision-making body of the Company             董事會為本公司的主要決策機構，負責監督本集團
and is responsible for overseeing the Group’s businesses,                的業務、戰略決策及表現，並透過指導及監督本公
strategic decisions and performance and is collectively responsible      司事務，共同負責促進本公司的成功。董事會客觀
for promoting the success of the Company by directing and                作出符合本公司利益的決策。全體董事（包括獨立非
supervising its affairs. The Board makes decisions objectively in        執行董事）為董事會帶來廣泛而寶貴的業務經驗、知
the interests of the Company. All Directors, including independent       識及專業精神，使其高效及有效地運作。
non-executive Directors, have brought a wide spectrum of valuable
business experience, knowledge and professionalism to the Board
for its efficient and effective functioning.

The day-to-day management, administration and operation of               本集團的日常管理、行政及營運乃授權予高級管理
the Group are delegated to the senior management. The Board              層負責。董事會定期檢討所委派的職能及職責。高
regularly reviews the functions and responsibilities delegated. The      級管理層於訂立任何重大交易前須取得董事會批准。
senior management needs to obtain approval from the Board prior
to entering into any significant transactions.

The Board reserves the right to make decisions on all substantial        董事會保留就本公司所有重大事宜作出決策的權
affairs of the Company, including: to approve and oversee all policy     利，包括：批准及監督所有政策及事宜、整體戰略
and matters, overall strategy and budgets, internal control and risk     及預算、內部監控及風險管理系統、重大交易（尤其
management systems, material transactions (in particular those that      是可能涉及利益衝突的交易）、財務資料、董事的委
may involve conflict of interests), financial information, appointment   任及其他主要財務及營運事宜。
of Directors and other major financial and operational issues.

The Company has adopted a policy to ensure independent views             本公司已採納一項政策，以確保董事會能獲得獨立
and inputs are available to the Board. The Directors may, at the         觀點及意見。董事可尋求其認為必要的獨立專業建
Company’s expense, seek independent professional advice, views           議、觀點及意見以履行其職責，及於根據有關政策
and input as considered necessary to fulfill their responsibilities      作出履行職責的決定時行使獨立判斷，費用由本公
and in exercising independent judgement when making decisions            司承擔。董事會將每年檢討有關機制的實施及成
in performing their duties according to such policy. The Board will      效。董事會已檢討報告期董事會獨立機制的實施及
review the implementation and effectiveness of such mechanism            成效，並認為其行之有效。
on an annual basis. The Board has reviewed the implementation

Nomination Policy                                                         提名政策
In identifying and selecting suitable candidates for directorships, the   於物色及甄選合適的董事候選人時，提名及企業管
Nominating and Corporate Governance Committee would consider              治委員會在向董事會作出推薦建議前，會考慮候選
the candidate’s relevant criteria that are necessary to complement        人的相關必備標準，以配合企業策略及達致董事會
the corporate strategy and achieve diversity of the Board, where          多元化（如適用）。
appropriate, before making recommendation to the Board.

Board Diversity Policy                                                    董事會多元化政策
The Board has adopted the Board Diversity Policy which sets out           董事會已採納董事會多元化政策，當中載列達致董
the approach to achieve diversity of the Board. The Board reviews         事會多元化的方法。董事會每年檢討董事會多元化

17 of the Listing Rules. The Company relies on the transitional        2023年1月1日起生效）所規定的過渡安排。
arrangements provided for under the new Chapter 17 of the Listing
Rules which became effective on January 1, 2023.

Purpose                                                                目的

and retain qualified personnel, provide incentives to employees,       激勵本集團僱員、顧問及董事，促進我們的業務取
consultants and directors of our Group, and promote the success        得成功。
of our business.

AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE                                     核數師就審計綜合財務報表須承擔的責
AUDIT OF THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                                    任
STATEMENTS
Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標，是就綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from         於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述獲取合理保
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to           證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。本報告僅
issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. Our report is     向 閣下整體報告，不作其他用途。我們概不就本
made solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do         報告內容對任何其他人士負責或承擔法律責任。
not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other
person for the contents of this report.

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a        合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按《香港審計
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             準則》進行的審計總能發現存在的重大錯誤陳述。
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期該
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         等錯誤陳述單獨或匯總起來可能影響該等綜合財務
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably   報表使用者依賴該等綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決
be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on      定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise              在根據《香港審計準則》進行審計的過程中，我們運
professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism            用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。我們亦：
throughout the audit. We also:

•    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the     •     識別及評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合財務報
     consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or              表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及執行審計
     error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to                程序以應對這些風險，以及獲取充足和適當的
     those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and           審計憑證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可
     appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of             能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或
     not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is           凌駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐而
     higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve          導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯

     or the override of internal control.

                      any unrealized profits arising from intra-group                        綜合財務報表時均全數抵銷。集團

                      acquisition basis, the Group chooses to apply                            購的資產總額的大部分公允價值都
                      a simplified assessment of whether an acquired                           集中於單一的可辨認資產或類似可
                      set of activities and assets is an asset rather                          辨認資產組別中時，本集團選擇採
                      than business acquisition, when substantially all                        用簡化的評估方法以確定所收購的
                      of the fair value of the gross assets acquired is                        一組活動和資產是否為資產而非業
                      concentrated in a single identifiable asset or group                     務收購。
                      of similar identifiable assets.

                      When a group of assets acquired and liabilities                          當取得的一組資產和承擔的負債不
                      assumed do not constitute a business, the overall                        構成業務時，按照收購日的相對公
                      acquisition cost is allocated to the individual                          允價值，將整體收購成本分攤至單
                      identifiable assets and liabilities based on their                       項可辨認資產和負債。可辨認資產
                      relative fair values at the date of acquisition. An                      和負債的個別公允價值之和不同於
                      exception is when the sum of the individual fair                         整體收購成本時除外。在該等情況
                      values of the identifiable assets and liabilities                        下，任何成本以外的金額根據本集
                      differs from the overall acquisition cost. In such                       團的政策進行初始計量的可辨認資
                      case, any identifiable assets and liabilities that                       產和負債均應進行相應計量，剩餘
                      are initially measured at an amount other than                           收購成本根據其在收購日的相對公
                      cost in accordance with the Group’s policies are                         允價值分配給剩餘的可辨認資產和
                      measured accordingly, and the residual acquisition                       負債。
                      cost is allocated to the remaining identifiable
                      assets and liabilities based on their relative fair

                      When acquiring assets by obtaining a controlling                         當通過獲得不構成分步收購業務的

        (g)    Leased assets                                                       (g)   租賃資產
               At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                    在合約開始時，本集團會評估該合約是
               the contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or                  否屬於或包含租賃。倘一份合約轉移於
               contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to                    一段時間內使用已確認資產的控制權以
               control the use of an identified asset for a period of                    換取對價，則該合約屬於或包含租賃。
               time in exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed                   如客戶既有權指示已確定資產的使用，
               where the customer has both the right to direct the use                   又有權從該使用中獲得絕大部分經濟利
               of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all of                益，則控制權被轉移。
               the economic benefits from that use.

               As a lessee                                                               作為承租人
               Where the contract contains lease component(s) and                        當合約包含租賃組成部分和非租賃組成
               non-lease component(s), the Group has elected not                         部分時，本集團選擇不將非租賃組成部
               to separate non-lease components and accounts for                         分分開，並將每個租賃組成部分和任何
               each lease component and any associated non-lease                         關聯的非租賃組成部分作為所有租賃的
               components as a single lease component for all leases.                    單一租賃組成部分進行列賬。

               At the lease commencement date, the Group recognizes                      在租賃開始日期，本集團確認使用權資
               a right-of-use asset and a lease liability, except for short-             產和租賃負債，但不包括租賃期不超過
               term leases that have a lease term of 12 months or less                   12個月的短期租賃和低價值資產租賃。
               and leases of low-value assets. When the Group enters                     當本集團就一項低價值資產訂立租約
               into a lease in respect of a low-value asset, the Group                   時，本集團決定是否以逐項租賃為基準
               decides whether to capitalize the lease on a lease-by-                    對該租賃進行資本化。與未資本化的租
               lease basis. The lease payments associated with those                     賃相關的租賃付款在租賃期內系統地確
               leases which are not capitalized are recognized as an                     認為開支。
               expense on a systematic basis over the lease term.

                Measurement of ECLs                                                        預期信貸虧損的計量
                ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of                                預期信貸虧損是以概率加權估計的
                credit losses. Credit losses are measured as the                           信貸虧損。信貸虧損按所有預期現
                present value of all expected cash shortfalls (i.e.                        金差額（即根據合約應付本集團的
                the difference between the cash flows due to the                           現金流與本集團預期收取現金流之
                Group in accordance with the contract and the                              間的差額）的現值計量。
                cash flows that the Group expects to receive).

                The expected cash shortfalls are discounted using                          如折現的影響重大，則預期現金差
                the following discount rates where the effect of                           額將按以下折現率折現：
                discounting is material:

                •    fixed-rate financial assets and trade and                             •      固定利率金融資產和貿易及
                     other receivables: effective interest rate                                   其他應收款項：初始確認時
                     determined at initial recognition or an                                      確定的實際利率或其近似值。
                     approximation thereof.

                The maximum period considered when estimating                              估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮的最長
                ECLs is the maximum contractual period over                                期限為本集團面臨信貸風險的最長
                which the Group is exposed to credit risk.                                 合約期。

                In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account                            在計量預期信貸虧損時，本集團考
                reasonable and supportable information that                                慮合理及有理據而無需付出過多的
                is available without undue cost or effort. This                            成本或努力即可獲得的資料，包括
                includes information about past events, current                            過往事件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀
                conditions and forecasts of future economic                                況預測等資料。
                conditions.

                ECLs are measured on either of the following                               預期信貸虧損基於以下基準計量：
                bases:

                •    12-month ECLs: these are losses that are                              •      12個月的預期信貸虧損：預
                     expected to result from possible default                                     計在報告日期後12個月內可
                     events within the 12 months after the                                        能發生的違約事件造成的虧
                     reporting date; and                                                          損；及

                •    lifetime ECLs: these are losses that are                              •      整個存續期的預期信貸虧
                     expected to result from all possible default                                 損：預計該等採用預期信貸
                     events over the expected lives of the items                                  虧損模式的項目在整個預期
                     to which the ECL model applies.                                              存續期內所有可能發生的違
                                                                                                  約事件造成的虧損。

                     Significant increases in credit risk                                        信貸風險顯著增加
                     In assessing whether the credit risk of a financial                         在評估金融工具的信貸風險自初始
                     instrument has increased significantly since initial                        確認後是否顯著增加時，本集團將
                     recognition, the Group compares the risk of default                         於報告日期評估的金融工具的違約
                     occurring on the financial instrument assessed at                           風險與於初始確認日評估的金融工
                     the reporting date with that assessed at the date                           具的違約風險進行比較。在進行此
                     of initial recognition. In making this reassessment,                        項重新評估時，本集團認為，當(i)
                     the Group considers that a default event occurs                             借款人不大可能在本集團不執行追
                     when (i) the borrower is unlikely to pay its credit                         索權採取變現抵押品（如持有）等
                     obligations to the Group in full, without recourse                          行動的情況下向本集團悉數支付其
                     by the Group to actions such as realizing security                          信貸債務；或(ii)金融資產逾期30
                     (if any is held); or (ii) the financial asset is 30 days                    天時，則構成違約事件。本集團會
                     past due. The Group considers both quantitative                             考慮合理及有理據的定量及定性資
                     and qualitative information that is reasonable                              料，包括歷史經驗及無需付出過多
                     and supportable, including historical experience                            的成本或努力即可獲得的前瞻性資
                     and forward-looking information that is available                           料。
                     without undue cost or effort.

          definition of a derivative; and the hybrid contract is not                   綜合損益表，則該衍生工具應與主體分
          measured at fair value through profit or loss. Embedded                      離，並作為獨立的衍生工具進行會計處
          derivatives are measured at fair value with changes in                       理。嵌入式衍生工具按公允價值計量，

          profit or loss. Reassessment occurs if there is a change                     約條款發生顯著改變現金流量之變更，
          in the terms of the contract that significantly modifies                     則須進行重新評估。
          the cash flows.

               Provisions are recognized when the Group has a legal                      當本集團有因過往事件而產生的法律或
               or constructive obligation arising as a result of a past                  推定義務，很可能需要產生經濟利益流
               event, it is probable that an outflow of economic benefits                出以清償該等義務，並作出可靠估計
               will be required to settle the obligation and a reliable                  時，確認撥備。倘貨幣的時間價值屬重
               estimate can be made. Where the time value of money                       大時，撥備乃按預計用於清償債務支出
               is material, provisions are stated at the present value of                的現值列示。
               the expenditure expected to settle the obligation.

               Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                      倘需要經濟利益流出的可能性不大，或
               benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                        者數額不能可靠估計，該義務應作為或
               estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                      有負債披露，除非經濟利益流出的可能
               contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow                   性極小。只有一項或多項未來事件的
               of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations,                     發生或不發生才能確認其存在的潛在義
               whose existence will only be confirmed by the                             務，亦作為或有負債披露，除非經濟利
               occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future                        益流出的可能性極小。
               events, are also disclosed as contingent liabilities
               unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is
               remote.

               are similar in respect of the nature of products and                   或提供服務的方法以至監管環境的本
               services, the type or class of customers, the methods                  質等經濟特性均屬類似，則作別論。個
               used to distribute the products or provide the services,               別非重要經營分部若滿足上述大部分標
               and the nature of the regulatory environment. Operating                準，則可以匯總。
               segments which are not individually material may be
               aggregated if they share a majority of these criteria.

    (continued)
    (z)   Redemption liabilities arising from preferred shares                    (z)    自優先股產生的贖回負債
          The Group designated the preferred shares with                                 本集團將本公司一間附屬公司發行的具
          redemption rights issued by a subsidiary of the Company                        有贖回權的優先股指定為以公允價值計
          as financial liabilities at fair value through profit or loss.                 量且其變動計入當期損益的金融負債。
          They are initially recognised at fair value. Any directly                      該等優先股於初始確認時按公允價值入
          attributable transaction costs are recognised in the                           賬。任何直接歸屬的交易成本均於綜合
          consolidated statements of profit or loss. Subsequent                          損益表中確認。於初始確認後，優先股
          to initial recognition, the preferred shares are carried at                    按公允價值列賬，其公允價值變動於綜
          fair value with changes in fair value recognised in the                        合損益表中確認，惟因本集團自身信貸
          consolidated statements of profit or loss, except for the                      風險所產生的損益除外，該等損益呈列
          gains or losses arising from the Group’s own credit risk                       於其他全面收益中，且其後不會重新分
          which are presented in other comprehensive income                              類至綜合損益表。
          with no subsequent reclassification to the consolidated

3   ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                              3      會計判斷及估計
    ESTIMATES
    The preparation of financial statements requires management                   編製該等財務報表需要管理層作出影響政策應
    to make judgements, estimates and assumptions that                            用以及資產、負債、收益及開支等呈報金額的
    affect the application of policies and reported amounts of                    判斷、估計及假設。該等估計及相關假設基於
    assets, liabilities, income and expenses. The estimates and                   過往經驗及在相關情況下被認為屬合理的多項
    associated assumptions are based on historical experience                     其他因素作出，其結果構成了對無法從其他途
    and various other factors that are believed to be reasonable                  徑輕易得知的資產及負債賬面值作出判斷的基
    under the circumstances, the results of which form the basis                  礎。實際業績可能有別於該等估計。
    of making the judgements about carrying values of assets
    and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other sources.
    Actual results may differ from these estimates.

    The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on                      該等估計和相關假設按持續基準予以審閱。會
    an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                       計估計的修訂是預先確定的。
    recognized prospectively.

    Significant judgements and estimates made in applying                         在採用會計政策時作出的重大判斷及估計載列
    accounting policies is as follows:                                            如下：


    Impairments of property, plant and equipment and                              與自營門店相關物業、廠房及設備以及使
    right-of-use assets related to self-operated stores                           用權資產的減值
    In considering the impairment losses that may be required                     在評估直營店相關若干物業、廠房及設備以及
    for certain property, plant and equipment and right-of-use                    使用權資產可能需計提之減值虧損時，須釐定
    assets related to self-operated stores, recoverable amount of                 該等資產之可收回金額，即公允價值減去出售
    these assets needs to be determined, being the higher of fair                 成本與使用價值兩者中的較高者。在確定這些
    value less costs of disposal and value in use. In determining                 資產的可收回金額時，本集團在估值專業人員
    the recoverable amount of these assets, the Group                             的協助下評估市場租金水平。
    evaluates market rental rates with the assistant of valuation

 5      REVENUE                                                           5   收入
        The Group’s revenue is primarily derived from the sale of             本集團的收入主要來自通過中國和海外的直營

               therefore, this investment is accounted for as a financial asset
               measured at FVTPL. The Group has an intention of holding such
               investment as a long-term investment.

 25 REDEMPTION LIABILITIES ARISING                                            25 自優先股產生的贖回負債
    FROM PREFERRED SHARES
        On July 24, 2025, a subsidiary of the Company, TOP TOY                  於 2025 年 7 月 24 日，本公司附屬公司 TOP
        International Group Limited (“TOP TOY”), entered into Series            TOY International Group Limited（「TOP
        A financing agreements with four Series A investors, pursuant           TOY」）與四名 A 輪投資者簽訂了 A 輪融資協
        to which, TOP TOY issued 47,632,778 Series A Preferred                  議，據此，TOP TOY於2025年7月31日以總
        Shares at an issue price totaling USD59,426,000 on July 31,             計59,426,000美元的發行價發行了47,632,778
        2025.                                                                   股A輪優先股。

        The key terms of the Series A Preferred Shares are                      A輪優先股的主要條款概述如下：
        summarized as follows:

        Redemption right                                                        贖回權
        The holders of Series A Preferred Shares may require TOP                A輪優先股持有人可於下列事件（「贖回事件」）
        TOY to redeem any or all of the Series A Preferred Shares               發生後，要求TOP TOY贖回其持有的任何或
        held by them at any time after the occurrence of the following          全部A輪優先股，以較早發生者為準：
        events (the “redemption events”) whichever is earlier:

        (i)     a qualified IPO of TOP TOY has not been consummated             (i)     TOP TOY未於相關A輪優先股發行日期
                on or prior to the third anniversary of the issue date with             起計第三週年之日或之前完成合資格首
                respect to the relevant Series A Preferred Shares;                      次公開發售；

        (ii)    TOP TOY or any of its affiliates is subject to any              (ii)    TOP TOY或其任何聯屬企業受到中國、
                sanctions enacted or enforced by the PRC, the United                    美國或其他相關國家、聯合國或歐盟頒
                States of America, or other relevant countries, or the                  佈或執行的任何制裁；或
                United Nations or the European Union; or

        (iii)   TOP TOY or the Controlling Shareholder has materially           (iii)   TOP TOY或控股股東已嚴重違反其各自
                breached any of their respective representations,                       的陳述、保證、契諾或承諾。
                warranties, covenants or undertakings.

        The redemption price shall be equal to the higher of (i) or (ii)        贖回價格應等於下列(i)或(ii)項中的較高者：(i)
        below: (i) the issue price plus an interest at the simple rate of       發行價格加上自發行日起至支付贖回價格之日
        ten percent per annum accruing from the issue date to the               止按年單利百分之十計算的利息；及(ii)相關A
        date of payment of the redemption price; and (ii) the fair value        輪優先股的公允價值。
        of respective Series A Preferred Shares.

               (i)     Merger reserve                                                    (i)     合併儲備
                       The merger reserve mainly represents the                                  合併儲備主要指先前同一控制下企
                       difference between the consideration paid and the                         業合併所支付的對價與取得的實收
                       paid-in capital acquired arising from a previous                          資本之間的差額。
                       business combination involving entities under
                       common control.

               (ii)    Translation reserve                                               (ii)    換算儲備
                       The exchange reserve comprises all foreign                                換算儲備包括換算國外業務財務報
                       exchange differences arising from the translation                         表產生的所有外匯差額。
                       of the financial statements of foreign operations.

                       granted to the key management personnel and                               本集團關鍵管理人員及僱員的受限
                       employees of the Group that has been recognized                           制股份、購股權及受限制股份單位
                       in accordance with the accounting policy adopted                          的授予日公允價值部分。
                       for share-based payments in Note 2(r)(iii).

               (iv)    PRC statutory reserve                                             (iv)    中國法定公積金

                       accordance with the PRC Company Law and the                               法》和在中國成立的附屬公司的組
                       Articles of Association of the subsidiaries which                         織章程細則而設立。如附屬公司為
                       are established in the PRC. The subsidiaries                              外商獨資企業或內資獨資企業，則
                       being wholly foreign-owned enterprise or wholly                           必須將其淨利潤的至少10%分配
                       domestic-owned enterprises, are required to                               至法定盈餘公積金。轉撥至法定盈
                       allocate at least 10% of its net profits to a statutory                   餘公積金必須在向權益股東分派股
                       surplus reserve. The transfer to this reserve must                        息之前作出。
                       be made before distribution of dividends to equity
                       shareholders can be made.

                       PRC statutory reserve can be used to make                                 中國法定公積金可用於彌補以往年
                       good previous years’ losses, if any, and may be                           度的虧損（如有），及可按其現有持
                       converted into capital in proportion to their existing                    股比例轉為資本，但轉為資本後的

30 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND                                    30 財務風險管理及公允價值
   FAIR VALUES
  Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency                本集團在日常業務過程中承受信貸、流動性、
  risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.               利率及貨幣風險。下文呈列本集團所承受的上
  The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk               述風險及本集團為管理該等風險所採用的財務
  management policies and practices used by the Group to                   風險管理政策及實務。
  manage these risks are described below.

  (a)   Credit risk                                                        (a)    信貸風險
        Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will                   信貸風險指交易對手未履行其合約責任
        default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                     而導致本集團產生財務虧損的風險。本
        financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is                   集團的信貸風險主要歸因於貿易及其他
        primarily attributable to trade and other receivables.                    應收款項。由於交易對手為本集團認為
        The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash                     信貸風險低且具有高信貸質量的銀行及
        and cash equivalents, restricted cash, term deposits                      金融機構，因此本集團承擔來自現金及
        and financial derivative assets is limited because the                    現金等價物、受限制現金、定期存款及
        counterparties are banks and financial institutions with                  金融衍生資產的信貸風險有限。
        high-credit-quality, for which the Group considers
        having low credit risk.

        Trade receivables                                                         貿易應收款項
        The Group’s trade receivables mainly derive from sales                    本集團的貿易應收款項主要來自於向代
        of goods to distributors and franchisees. The Group’s                     理商及加盟商銷售貨物。本集團面臨的
        exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by the                       信貸風險主要受每個客戶的個別特徵（而
        individual characteristics of each customer rather than                   非客戶經營所在的行業或國家）影響，
        the industry or country in which the customers operate                    因此，當本集團與個別客戶有大額往來
        and therefore significant concentrations of credit risk                   時，即面對信貸風險高度集中的情況。
        primarily arise when the Group has significant exposure                   於2024年及2025年12月31日，貿易應
        to individual customers. At December 31, 2024 and                         收款項總額的35%及32%分別來自本集
        2025, 35% and 32% of the total trade receivables were                     團的五大債務人。
        due from the Group’s five largest debtors, respectively.

        Individual credit evaluations are performed on all                        本集團會對所有要求獲得超出若干金額
        customers requiring credit over a certain amount. These                   的信貸的客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等
        evaluations focus on the customer’s history of making                     評估針對客戶到期還款記錄及現時還款
        payments when due and current ability to pay and take                     能力，並考慮客戶的具體信息以及與客
        into account information specific to the customer as well                 戶經營所在經濟環境有關的信息。
        as pertaining to the economic environment in which the
        customer operates.

        (c)    Interest rate risk                                                            (c)     利率風險
               Interest rate risk is the risk that the fair value or future                          利率風險是指金融工具的公允價值或未來現金
               cash flows of a financial instrument will fluctuate                                   流量因市場利率變動而波動的風險。於各報告
               because of changes in market interest rates. The Group                                期末，本集團並無將任何固定利率金融工具按
               does not account for any fixed-rate financial instruments                             公允價值計入損益。因此，按固定利率計息的
               at fair value through profit or loss at the end of each                               金融工具不會使本集團面臨公允價值利率風
               reporting period. Therefore, interest-bearing financial                               險。本集團的利率風險主要來自受限制現金及
               instruments at fixed rates do not expose the Group to                                 以浮動利率計息的銀行存款，這使本集團面臨
               fair value interest rate risk. The Group’s interest rate risk                         現金流量利率風險。本集團根據目前的市場情
               arises primarily from restricted cash and cash at bank                                況釐定固定利率和浮動利率計息工具的適當權
               at variable rates, which exposes the Group to cash flow                               重，並定期進行審查和監測，以實現固定利率
               interest rate risk. The Group determines the appropriate                              和浮動利率風險的適當組合。本集團並無訂立
               weightings of the fixed and floating rate interest-bearing                            金融衍生工具對沖其利率風險。
               instruments based on the current market conditions and
               performs regular reviews and monitoring to achieve an
               appropriate mix of fixed and floating rate exposure. The
               Group does not enter into financial derivatives to hedge
               interest rate risk.

                     in active markets for identical assets or                                      同資產或負債於活躍市場的
                     liabilities at the measurement date.                                           未經調整報價）計量的公允價

                     to meet Level 1, and not using significant                                     可觀察輸入數據）且並非使用
                     unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs                                       重大不可觀察輸入數據計量的
                     are inputs for which market data are not                                       公允價值。不可觀察輸入數據
                     available.                                                                     為無市場數據的輸入數據。

               •     Level 3: Fair value measured using significant                          •      第三層級：使用重大不可觀
                     unobservable inputs.                                                           察輸入數據計量的公允價值。

               operations and financials in violation of the Securities              務運營和財務方面存在誤導性錯誤陳述
               Act of 1933 and the Securities Exchange Act of 1934.                  或遺漏。訴訟標題為「關於名創優品集
               The action is captioned In re MINISO Group Holding                    團控股有限公司證券訴訟案」，1:22-cv-
               Limited Securities Litigation, 1:22-cv-09864 (S.D.N.Y.).              09864（ 紐約州南區法院）。首席原告於
               Lead plaintiff was appointed in November 2022 and filed               2022年11月獲委任，並向法院提交有效
               the operative complaint to the court. The company and                 訴狀。本公司和其他被告提交了駁回訴
               other defendants filed a motion to dismiss the complaint,             狀的動議，該動議於2024年2月獲法院
               and the motion was granted by the court in February                   批准，並准予修正。原告繼而提交了重
               2024, with leave to amend. Plaintiffs filed a motion                  新審議法院決定的動議，但已被法院駁

    (continued)
        The Group is in the process of making an assessment of                本集團目前正評估這些新訂及經修訂準則於首
        the impact of these new and amended standards upon                    次應用時所產生的影響。《國際財務報告準則》
        initial application. IFRS 18 introduces new requirements for          第18 號就損益表的列報引入了新規定，包括
        presentation within the statement of profit or loss, including        特定的總計及小計。實體須將損益表內的所有
        specified totals and subtotals. Entities are required to classify     收入及支出分類至以下五類之一：經營活動、
        all income and expenses within the statement of profit or             投資活動、融資活動、所得稅及終止營運，並
        loss into one of the five categories: operating, investing,           須列報兩項新定義的小計。該準則亦要求於附
        financing, income taxes and discontinued operations and to            註中披露管理層界定的績效指標，並就財務資
        present two new defined subtotals. It also requires disclosure        料的匯總與拆分提出新規定。預期新規定將影
        of management-defined performance measures in a note                  響本集團損益表的列報方式及本集團財務績效
        and introduces new requirements for aggregation and                   的披露。截至目前，本集團認為該等新訂及經
        disaggregation of financial information. The new requirements         修訂準則不大可能對本集團的經營業績及財務
        are expected to impact the Group’s presentation of the                狀況產生重大影響。
        statement of profit or loss and disclosures of the Group’s
        financial performance. So far, the Group considers that the
        new and revised standards are unlikely to have a significant
        impact on the Group’s results of operations and financial
        position.

 “Prospectus”               the prospectus of the Company dated June 30, 2022

 “Retail Partner”           franchisee under our Retail Partner model, a franchise-like store model with
                            chain store characteristics, where the franchisee bears the store opening
                            capital expenditure and store operating expenses to join our “MINISO” or
                            “TOP TOY” branded retail store franchise
「合夥人」                   指   合夥人模式下的加盟商，該模式具有連鎖加盟店特徵。在該模式下，加盟商將
                            為使用我們「名創優品」或「TOP TOY」品牌零售門店特許經營權承擔開店資本
                            開支和門店運營費用

                            administered municipalities, and other cities of China that the Company
                            considers to have a similar level of development potential

                            為具有相似水平發展潛力的其他城市
